                                                              f. The resuscitation procedure is as follows:
                                                                 (1) Exert downward pressure, not exceeding

                                                          60 pounds, for 1 second.

                                                                  (2) Swing back, suddenly releasing pressure,
                                                          and sit up on the heels.

                                                          pressure for another second.

?  CHAPTER 4

FIELD MAINTENANCE INSTRUCTIONS

   Note. This chapter contains information for field maintenance. The amount of repair that can be performed
by units having field maintenance responsibility is limited only by the tools and test equipment available, and
by the skill of the repairman.
